- **benchmark-design**: 为数据、查询或事务系统设计、写规范或审查一份评测负载或基准。框架来自 Jim Gray 的《The Benchmark Handbook for Database and Transaction Systems》（relevant（切合领域）、portable（易于移植）、scaleable（可随规模伸缩）、simple（简单易懂）；一份负载规范加上一份测量规范；教训来自 TPC-A/B/C/D、Wisconsin、AS3AP、Set Query 与 Engineering Database Benchmark）。凡是用户必须决定测什么、如何测时都要使用：选择或改适配基准，定义操作及其配比，构造数据生成器（偏斜、选择率、局部性），设定规模因子、指标、延迟分位数、成本归一化、运行规则、正确性检查、调优与公平性规则，或撰写论文的实验设置；以及每当他们询问一份评测计划是否公平或有说服力时（例如「我们每个引擎只跑一次 TPC-H，这样够吗？」）。即使用户没有说出 Gray、handbook 或 benchmark 这些词也要触发，例如「我该如何评测我的引擎」「我该用什么负载」。 ([Aethergrids/Metis](https://github.com/Aethergrids/Metis/tree/HEAD/plugins/dias-kit-zh/skills/benchmark-design))

- **beni**: Write, read or review beni code. beni is an Elm-like language that compiles to JavaScript, and Elm code fails in it. There is no currying (every call is saturated, `f a _` is partial application), static dispatch (`x.m a`, `where` clauses), array-backed lists written `[ x, …rest ]` with no `::`, Unicode notation (`→ ← ≠ ≤ ≥ ▷ ◁ …`), `⊤` and `⊥` for `()` and `Never`, `λ` lambdas, blocks instead of `let … in`, and inferred effects with no `Task e a` type. Read this skill before you write or change a `.beni` file, a `beni.json` or a `beni` command line, also when you know Elm well. ([canassa/beni](https://github.com/canassa/beni/tree/HEAD/skills/beni) · ★42)

- **benjamin-graham-perspective**: 本杰明·格雷厄姆（Benjamin Graham, 1894-1976，"价值投资之父"、Buffett 的老师）的思维框架与决策系统。 基于《Security Analysis》(1934)、《The Intelligent Investor》(1949)、1976 FAJ 最后访谈、 1976 Butler 口述访谈、哥大 Phi Beta Kappa 古典学训练、Graham-Newman 20 年实战记录、 Zweig 注释版、Carlen/Lowe/Lowenstein 三本传记、Munger/Buffett/Klarman/Bogle/Fama-French 等 80+ 一手与学术来源深度调研，提炼 5 个核心心智模型、9 条决策启发式、完整表达 DNA 与 Graham 最罕见的「晚年自我颠覆」张力。 用途：作为投资与决策顾问，用 Graham 的镜片审视股票估值、投资决策、投机 vs 投资边界、 面对市场情绪的反应、面对不确定性的方法。 当用户提到「用格雷厄姆的视角」「Graham 会怎么看」「价值投资之父」「Mr. Market」 「市场先生」「安全边际」「Margin of Safety」「内在价值」「Net-Net」「烟蒂股」 「聪明的投资者」「Security Analysis」「证券分析」「Graham-Newman」「防御型投资者」时使用。 也适用于：… ([OpenSucker/OpenSucker](https://github.com/OpenSucker/OpenSucker/tree/HEAD/skills/personas/graham) · ★16 · 2 copies)
- **benjamin-graham-perspective**: 本杰明·格雷厄姆(Benjamin Graham)的思维框架与表达方式。基于著作、访谈、学生回忆等多源深度调研， 提炼6个核心心智模型、10条决策启发式和完整的表达DNA。 被誉为"价值投资之父"、"证券分析之父"， Warren Buffett的老师。 用途：作为思维顾问，用Graham的视角分析投资决策、审视风险、提供保守主义建议。 当用户提到「用Graham的视角」「格雷厄姆会怎么看」「价值投资视角」「安全边际」「市场先生」时使用。 即使用户只是说「这笔投资安全吗」「价格合理吗」「怎么看市场波动」也应触发。 ([robertquant/nuwa-skill](https://github.com/robertquant/nuwa-skill/tree/HEAD/benjamin-graham-perspective) · ★4)
- **benjamini-hochberg-fdr-correction**: Use when novelty scores (reconstruction error, Mahalanobis distance, IF/LOF/SVM scores) have been converted to empirical P-values against a B-DNA null and a per-window significance decision is needed at a fixed FDR level. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/benjamini-hochberg-fdr-correction--34292fcc0c822f93))
- **benjamini-hochberg-fdr-correction**: Use when testing thousands of phased heterozygous SNPs for allelic imbalance in ChIA-PET read coverage and needing multiple-testing-corrected significance calls before classifying allele-specific loops. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/benjamini-hochberg-fdr-correction--67b213a327299042))
- **benjamini-hochberg-fdr-correction**: Use when you have a vector of per-region p-values (e.g. Fisher's exact tests of methylated vs unmethylated CpG counts per haplotype) and must control the expected fraction of false allele-specific calls at 10% FDR before reporting final region counts. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/benjamini-hochberg-fdr-correction--63abb4bdac81c45e))
- **benjamini-hochberg-fdr-correction**: Use when p-values from CHEUI-diff's per-site Mann-Whitney U-tests (or a comparable differential nanopore m6A test) need Benjamini-Hochberg FDR correction before counting significant sites at alpha = 0.05, 0.01, and 0.001. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/benjamini-hochberg-fdr-correction--873fef70e9393767))
- **benjamini-hochberg-fdr-correction**: Use when you have feature-wise p-values from univariate tests (Welch's t-test, Mann-Whitney U, or one-way ANOVA) across a large LC-MS metabolomics feature table and need multiplicity-corrected q-values to select candidate biomarkers. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/benjamini-hochberg-fdr-correction--2f20ac4963dc6fb2))
- **benjamini-hochberg-fdr-correction**: Use when a spatial metabolomics feature matrix (e.g., 14,260 pixels x 3,044 ion peaks) has been grouped into two regions (e.g., pixel clusters MB vs HIP) and every ion's raw p-value from a univariate test must be multiplicity-corrected before calling differential metabolites. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/benjamini-hochberg-fdr-correction--e16a44774183cc81))
- **benjamini-hochberg-fdr-correction**: Use when a table of per-reaction concordance scores (Cohen's kappa, Pearson correlation of RPS vs RAS and RPS vs FFD) needs multiple-comparison correction to decide which reactions are statistically significantly concordant at 5% FDR. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/benjamini-hochberg-fdr-correction--a71f9a3141ba6352))
- **benjamini-hochberg-fdr-correction**: Use when a MOFA factor-interpretation step yields many nominal p-values from Pearson (numeric clinical features) and Wilcoxon (binary clinical features) tests that must be FDR-corrected before ranking discriminant factors. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/benjamini-hochberg-fdr-correction--bd1382873401fda6))
- **benjamini-hochberg-fdr-correction**: Use when a feature table from LC/MS preprocessing (apLCMS, XCMS, or two-stage apLCMS, batch-corrected or not) has per-feature t-test p-values between two cell types and you need multiplicity-adjusted significance counts at a lenient FDR cutoff such as 0.2. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/benjamini-hochberg-fdr-correction--fa1b358e7526a8b5))
- **benjamini-hochberg-fdr-correction**: Use when evaluating one-mismatch children from HashSeq's OneMismatchCluster.txt against a background error rate with poisson.test, and per-child P values must be multiple-hypothesis corrected with BH at 5% FDR before writing InferenceTestSignificantChildren.txt. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/benjamini-hochberg-fdr-correction--7e6b57b09bec1a6b))
- **benjamini-hochberg-fdr-correction**: Use when comparing case-control metagenomic feature tables (e.g. healthy vs adenoma vs carcinoma CSS-normalized abundance at five annotation levels) and you need per-feature P-values corrected for multiple testing. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/benjamini-hochberg-fdr-correction--3704150ef29a9ece))
- **benjamini-hochberg-fdr-correction**: Use when you have per-feature or per-group p-values (e.g., from VI-VS CRT with K Monte Carlo replicates) and need to call discoveries at target FDR levels such as alpha=0.1 while controlling the realized false discovery rate. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics/tree/HEAD/collections/multi-omics/v1/leaves/benjamini-hochberg-fdr-correction))
- **benjamini-hochberg-fdr-correction**: Use when a differential expression meta-analysis yields one combined P value per gene per cell subtype and you must control multiplicity before ranking marker genes (e.g., recovering Sst, Pvalb, Vip, Cck, Htr3a as subtype discriminators). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/benjamini-hochberg-fdr-correction--119a1169934abe9b))
- **benjamini-hochberg-fdr-correction**: Use when you have raw p-values from multiple testing methods (e.g., progressionTest, fateSelectionTest, DAseq, milo) on the same features/genes and need binary differential calls at a controlled false discovery rate of 5% for downstream performance evaluation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/benjamini-hochberg-fdr-correction--9ab4d1f0c66ffbb3))
- **benjamini-hochberg-fdr-correction**: Use when a gene-list enrichment run (E.PAGE compute/compute_sets over the curated 522-module, 25,789-gene database) produces many raw hypergeometric p-values that must be adjusted for multiple hypothesis testing before filtering significant environmental modules. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/benjamini-hochberg-fdr-correction--d3a6acb1d4e9a7c1))
- **benjamini-hochberg-fdr-correction**: Use when you have raw per-gene p-values from a two-group differential expression test (e.g. a Wilcoxon rank-sum test between user-defined cell groups) and need FDR-corrected values before ranking marker genes. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/benjamini-hochberg-fdr-correction--2a14a43426fc3e0d))
- **benjamini-hochberg-fdr-correction**: Use when many candidate biomarkers (e.g., 16 gene expression programs or transferred qPHATE coordinates) each yield a Cox regression treatment-effect contrast and the family of p-values must be FDR-corrected before declaring significance. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/benjamini-hochberg-fdr-correction--1728193975e54611))
- **benjamini-hochberg-fdr-edge-significance-testing**: Use when constructing a multi-omics co-occurrence network from paired feature tables (e. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/benjamini-hochberg-fdr-edge-significance-testing))
- **benjamini-hochberg-fdr-estimation**: Use when testing differential regulatory relations between two conditions from paired single-cell data and needing calibrated p-values/FDR for the likelihood ratio statistic, especially when the standard Chi-square null appears anti-conservative. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/benjamini-hochberg-fdr-estimation))
- **benjamini-hochberg-fdr-multiple-testing-correction**: Use when a statistical test (e.g., a linear mixed model with diet as fixed effect) has been run independently for many taxa or features, such as hundreds of SGBs, and raw p-values must be converted into BH-adjusted FDR values before declaring biomarkers. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding/tree/HEAD/collections/metabarcoding/v1/leaves/benjamini-hochberg-fdr-multiple-testing-correction))
- **benjamini-hochberg-fdr-multiple-testing-correction**: Use when hundreds/thousands of taxa (phylum through genus) each yield a raw P-value from group-comparison regression and a family-wise-error-controlled, FDR-adjusted significance list is needed. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/benjamini-hochberg-fdr-multiple-testing-correction))
- **benjamini-hochberg-fdr-multiple-testing-correction**: Use when PAIRADISE's Step 5 statistical model (LRT on delta_i) has produced thousands of raw event-level p-values and you must control the false discovery fraction across tests to call ASAS event-SNP pairs at FDR &lt;= 10%. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/benjamini-hochberg-fdr-multiple-testing-correction--fa2181d8037cba15))
- **benjamini-hochberg-fdr-multiple-testing-correction**: Use when many parallel hypothesis tests must be thresholded with false-discovery-rate control — e.g., hundreds of GO over-representation tests on top-ranked communities per donor per time point — and an adjusted-p (BH FDR 0.05) significance call is required. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/benjamini-hochberg-fdr-multiple-testing-correction--58a316a4fb3be2ab))
- **benjamini-hochberg-multiple-testing-adjustment**: Use when a hypothesis-testing co-expression method (CS-CORE, Pearson, Spearman, locCSN, normalisr, NoiseRegularization) yields one p-value per gene pair across a p x p matrix, and the network must be thresholded or PR curves computed. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/benjamini-hochberg-multiple-testing-adjustment))
- **benjamini-hochberg-multiple-testing-correction**: Use when a family of hypothesis tests has been run per cluster (cluster-vs-rest Wilcoxon tests across regions, or hypergeometric tests across MSigDB v5 gene sets) and raw p-values must be adjusted before thresholding significant regions or pathways. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/benjamini-hochberg-multiple-testing-correction))
- **benjamini-hochberg-multiple-testing-correction**: Use when per-term p-values from S2IsoMEr bootstrapping ORA/MSEA (one-tailed Fisher's exact tests over &gt;=50-1000 bootstrap iterations) must be FDR-controlled before reporting overrepresented HMDB/RAMP-DB or LION terms. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/benjamini-hochberg-multiple-testing-correction--51fdccebe8149db6))
- **benjamini-hochberg-multiple-testing-correction**: Use when a metabolomics pathway-enrichment step yields one raw p-value per pathway (e.g. from Fisher's Exact Test on 2x2 contingency tables or fgsea MetSEA) and pathways must be ranked by adjusted p-value with a significance cutoff (adjusted p &lt; 0.05). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/benjamini-hochberg-multiple-testing-correction--4ab6f1155a5ff668))
- **benjamini-hochberg-multiple-testing-correction**: Use when a differential analysis of grouped NMR peak features produces thousands of per-feature p-values and you need FDR-controlled significance calls comparable across frameworks such as speaq 2.0 and MetaboAnalyst. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/benjamini-hochberg-multiple-testing-correction--167cbe6451059b92))
- **benjamini-hochberg-multiple-testing-correction**: Use when a metabolomics (or other omics) feature table requires a per-feature significance test (e.g. linear regression for diet/group effect) across thousands of features and raw p-values must be adjusted before selecting differential features with cutoffs such as Log2FC &gt; 1.5 and adj-p &lt; 0.05. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/benjamini-hochberg-multiple-testing-correction--9ce95e84386da810))
- **benjamini-hochberg-multiple-testing-correction**: Use when many metabolite features (e.g. bile-amidate feature rows in a relative-quantification table) have each been tested with a Wilcoxon rank-sum test, producing a family of raw p-values that must be FDR-adjusted before calling disease associations. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/benjamini-hochberg-multiple-testing-correction--26280cd418d45c3b))
- **benjamini-hochberg-multiple-testing-correction**: Use when many parallel significance tests have been run (e.g. one hypergeometric GO over-representation test per bicluster across 151 UniBic biclusters) and enrichment calls must control the false discovery rate before tallying proportions. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/benjamini-hochberg-multiple-testing-correction--c12de7edc771dfa4))
- **benjamini-hochberg-multiple-testing-correction**: Use when hundreds of module-vs-term hypergeometric tests are performed (e.g., 137 MCA GEPs against GO/MP gene sets) and enriched terms must be reported with BH-adjusted p-values alongside raw p-values and enrichment ratios. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/benjamini-hochberg-multiple-testing-correction--8e3821f3f12b38e1))
- **benjamini-hochberg-multiple-testing-correction**: Use when NeuronChat (or analogous permutation-based cell-cell communication inference) has produced raw per-interaction p-values across many ligand-receptor pairs and you need FDR-controlled significance calls before retaining or zeroing links. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/benjamini-hochberg-multiple-testing-correction--9474715561b1608a))
- **benjamini-hochberg-multiple-testing-correction**: Use when many gene-set (GO_BP/KEGG) enrichment tests are run in parallel — e.g., one test per detected RGM or SCENIC regulon on the hESC, mESC, or mHSC lineage datasets — and per-module significance must be assessed with a threshold that accounts for multiple testing. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/benjamini-hochberg-multiple-testing-correction--66d8776173f96f0e))
- **benjamini-hochberg-multiple-testing-correction**: Use when a single analysis produces many pathway-enrichment p-values (e.g. 100 scETM topics tested against MSigDB Canonical Pathways or PsyGeNET gene sets) and significant topic-pathway associations must be controlled for false discovery. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/benjamini-hochberg-multiple-testing-correction--11ab09a05854c201))
- **benjamini-hochberg-multiple-testing-correction**: Use when you have thousands of per-gene isoform (or TSS/polyA) chi-square p-values from a scisorseqr region-vs-region comparison and need FDR-controlled DIE calls with an effect-size (delta-PI) filter. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/benjamini-hochberg-multiple-testing-correction--82ef6cc8852f7587))
- **benjamini-hochberg-multiple-testing-correction**: Use when a gene-wise statistical test (e.g., limma moderated t-statistics across 46 cases and 23 controls) yields thousands of raw P-values that must be FDR-adjusted before ranking seeds or thresholding DEGs. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/benjamini-hochberg-multiple-testing-correction--4304745954c61c52))
- **benjamini-hochberg-multiple-testing-correction**: Use when a gene-set activity analysis tests many gene sets simultaneously and raw empirical p-values (e.g. ppv L1, ppv Median Exp from rROMA) must be adjusted before classifying modules as significant. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/benjamini-hochberg-multiple-testing-correction--246a6e68cd4f4915))
- **benjamini-hochberg-multiple-testing-correction**: Use when many paired statistical tests (e.g., matched perturbed-vs-control Wilcoxon tests over hundreds of TF-gene edges) have been run and raw p-values must be FDR-adjusted before calling significant edges. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/benjamini-hochberg-multiple-testing-correction--413d544d478653a0))
- **benjamini-hochberg-multiple-testing-correction**: Use when many hypothesis tests (e.g., GSEA on 22 KEGG-derived LR pathway sets per factor) produce raw P-values per condition, and FDR-controlled adjusted P-values are needed for a factor-by-pathway results table. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/benjamini-hochberg-multiple-testing-correction--868b86a246af3f9a))
- **benjamini-hochberg-multiple-testing-correction**: Use when a tncRNA count matrix with ≥3 biological replicates per condition is being tested for differential expression across many tncRNA loci and per-locus p-values must be corrected for multiple testing before significance calling. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/benjamini-hochberg-multiple-testing-correction--9e0f23741240da3a))
- **benjamini-hochberg-pvalue-adjustment**: Use when a per-cluster marker gene list has been tested against cell-type gene sets (e.g. easyct with test='fisher') and the family of candidate cell-type p-values within each cluster must be FDR-adjusted before ranking annotations. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/benjamini-hochberg-pvalue-adjustment))
- **benjamini-hochberg-pvalue-correction**: Use when running MetaboDiff diff_test on a metabolite measurement table with two or more groups (e.g. MYC-high vs AKT1-high), where raw Student's t-test or ANOVA p-values must be adjusted for multiple testing across metabolites. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/benjamini-hochberg-pvalue-correction))
- **benjamini-hochberg-pvalue-correction**: Use when many per-gene hypothesis tests (e.g. Fisher's exact tests on gene-cluster absence/presence per lifestyle) yield a vector of p-values that must be FDR-corrected before ranking candidate lifestyle-associated genes. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/benjamini-hochberg-pvalue-correction))
- **benjamini-yekutieli-fdr-correction**: Use when a family of P-values is large and potentially dependent (e.g. MEPP positional correlations at adjacent sequence positions, or all motifs at all positions) and a dependency-robust FDR adjustment is required. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/benjamini-yekutieli-fdr-correction))
- **benjamini-yekutieli-fdr-correction**: Use when a large, dependent family of p-values (e.g. hypergeometric tests for gene-pair adjacency or co-localisation in BGCs) must be FDR-corrected before thresholding, as in PRESTO-STAT sub-cluster detection. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/benjamini-yekutieli-fdr-correction))
- **benji-taylor-design-eval**: Use when reviewing, critiquing, or auditing a product's design quality, UX, or interface decisions. - **benkyo-project-init**: Set up a new benkyo learning project, or resume a project after a long gap. Use this skill whenever the learner says they want to start studying a subject ("○○を勉強したい", "○○の試験勉強", "○○ができるようになりたい"), shares course materials (textbook, lecture recordings, past exams, syllabus), expresses a learning goal without yet having a benkyo project, or returns to an existing project after a long break. Trigger when no benkyo project exists for the topic, OR when the learner needs help defining what to learn. Also trigger when the learner is goal-less ("何か勉強したい") and needs help formulating a concrete target. - **benkyo-session-wrap**: Wrap up a benkyo learning session cleanly and prepare for resumption. Use this skill when the learner says 「今日はここまで」「終わり」「また明日」「疲れた」「時間ない」「もう寝る」, or signals they're about to stop. Also trigger when you (the tutor) notice the natural completion of a topic and want to propose a stopping point, or when a session has gone long enough that a wrap is appropriate. This skill handles the recap, delayed JOL seeding, and persistence of session state through the events log so the next session can resume cleanly. - **benkyo-treatment-shift**: Change the depth-of-engagement (blackbox vs whitebox treatment) for a concept in a benkyo project. Use this skill when the learner expresses 「ちゃんと理解したい」「腹落ちしたい」「導出やりたい」「なぜそうなる？」「もっと深く」 (commit signals — wants deeper understanding), or 「公式覚えれば」「暗記でいい」「ざっくり」「飛ばして」「もういい」 (release signals — wants to shortcut to memorization). Also trigger when you (the tutor) detect indirect signals: repeated "なんで" on a concept, mis-applying a blackbox reference, failed transfer to varied problems (commit candidates), or stuck-multiple-breakdowns / fatigue / time-pressure (release candidates). - **benkyo-tutoring**: Conduct learning sessions using the benkyo CLI.
